    <!-- Embed Modal -->
    <div id="embed-modal" class="fixed inset-0 z-[100] hidden flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <!-- Backdrop -->
        <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm" onclick="closeEmbedModal()"></div>
        
        <!-- Modal Content -->
        <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-3xl flex flex-col relative z-10 overflow-hidden border border-gray-200">
            <!-- Header -->
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center p-4 border-b border-gray-100 bg-gray-50">
                <h3 class="text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <svg class="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M10 20l4-16m4 4l4 4-4 4M6 16l-4-4 4-4" /></svg>
                    Component Resources
                </h3>
                <button onclick="closeEmbedModal()" class="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-200 p-2 rounded-lg transition-colors">
                    <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12" /></svg>
                </button>
            </div>
            
            <!-- Tabs -->
            <div class="flex border-b border-gray-100 px-2 bg-gray-50 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide">
                <button onclick="switchTab('html')" id="tab-btn-html" class="px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 border-indigo-600 text-indigo-600 whitespace-nowrap">HTML Code</button>
                <button onclick="switchTab('css')" id="tab-btn-css" class="px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 whitespace-nowrap">CSS Styles</button>
                <button onclick="switchTab('iframe')" id="tab-btn-iframe" class="px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 whitespace-nowrap">Iframe Embed</button>
                <button onclick="switchTab('prompt')" id="tab-btn-prompt" class="px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold border-b-2 border-transparent text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 whitespace-nowrap flex items-center gap-1">
                    <svg class="w-4 h-4 text-purple-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13 10V3L4 14h7v7l9-11h-7z"></path></svg>
                    AI Prompt
                </button>
            </div>
            
            <!-- Content Area -->
            <div class="p-4 bg-gray-900 relative group h-96 overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
                <button onclick="copyCurrentCode()" class="absolute top-6 right-6 bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-colors border border-white/20 shadow-lg flex items-center gap-2 z-10 backdrop-blur-sm">
                    <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M8 16H6a2 2 0 01-2-2V6a2 2 0 012-2h8a2 2 0 012 2v2m-6 12h8a2 2 0 002-2v-8a2 2 0 00-2-2h-8a2 2 0 00-2 2v8a2 2 0 002 2z"></path></svg>
                    Copy Code
                </button>
                <textarea id="embed-code-area" readonly class="w-full h-full bg-transparent text-green-400 font-mono text-sm p-4 resize-none focus:outline-none scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-gray-700"></textarea>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Embed Logic -->
        <script>
        let currentModalData = { iframe: '', html: '', css: '', prompt: '' };
        let currentTab = 'html';

        function openEmbedModal(id) {
            document.getElementById('embed-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
            
            const section = document.getElementById(id);
            if(!section) return;

            const grid = section.querySelector('.grid');
            let rawHtml = '';
            if(grid && grid.children.length > 1) {
                const showcasePart = grid.children[1];
                rawHtml = showcasePart.innerHTML.trim();
            } else {
                rawHtml = section.innerHTML.trim();
            }

            rawHtml = rawHtml.replace(/<button[^>]*onclick="openEmbedModal[^>]*>.*?<\/button>/gis, '').trim();

            let cssText = '';
            const allClasses = new Set();
            
            section.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(el => {
                el.classList.forEach(c => {
                    if(c.includes('-') && !c.includes('text-') && !c.includes('bg-') && !c.includes('hover:') && !c.includes('w-') && !c.includes('h-') && !c.includes('p-') && !c.includes('m-') && !c.includes('border') && !c.includes('rounded') && !c.includes('shadow') && !c.includes('flex') && !c.includes('grid')) {
                        allClasses.add(c);
                    }
                });
            });
            
            section.classList.forEach(c => allClasses.add(c));

            const styleSheets = document.querySelectorAll('style');
            styleSheets.forEach(sheet => {
                if(!sheet.textContent) return;
                const rules = sheet.textContent.split('}');
                rules.forEach(rule => {
                    const trimmed = rule.trim();
                    if(trimmed) {
                        for(let c of allClasses) {
                            if(trimmed.includes('.' + c)) {
                                cssText += '        ' + trimmed.replace(/\n/g, '\n        ') + '}\n\n';
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });
            });
            
            if(!cssText) cssText = '        /* No custom CSS needed. Pure Tailwind! */\n';
            currentModalData.css = cssText.trim();
            
            const sectionClass = section.className.replace(/relative/g, '').replace(/group/g, '').replace(/min-h-screen/g, '').replace(/flex items-center justify-center/g, '').replace(/py-\d+/g, '').replace(/px-\d+/g, '').trim();

            const fullHtml = [
                '<!DOCTYPE html>',
                '<html lang="en">',
                '<head>',
                '    <meta charset="UTF-8">',
                '    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">',
                '    <title>UI Component - ' + id + '</title>',
                '    <!-- Tailwind CSS -->',
                '    <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"><' + '/script>',
                '    <!-- Google Fonts -->',
                '    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Caveat:wght@700&family=Silkscreen&family=Space+Mono:ital,wght@0,400;0,700;1,400&family=VT323&family=Outfit:wght@300;500;800&family=Righteous&family=Playfair+Display:ital,wght@0,400;0,800;1,400&family=Courier+Prime:wght@400;700&family=Shrikhand&family=Bangers&family=Inter:wght@400;700;900&family=Creepster&family=Berkshire+Swash&family=Monoton&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">',
                '    <style>',
                '        body { font-family: \'Outfit\', sans-serif; }',
                cssText,
                '    </style>',
                '</' + 'head>',
                '<body class="antialiased text-gray-800 selection:bg-black selection:text-white">',
                '    <!-- Component Code -->',
                '    <div class="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center p-8 ' + sectionClass + '">',
                '        ' + rawHtml,
                '    </div>',
                '</' + 'body>',
                '</' + 'html>'
            ].join('\n');

            currentModalData.html = fullHtml;

            // Generate clean srcdoc iframe instead of hash URL to prevent scroll jumping
            const encodedHtml = fullHtml.replace(/'/g, '&apos;');
            currentModalData.iframe = `<iframe srcdoc='${encodedHtml}' width="100%" height="600" style="border:none; border-radius:16px; box-shadow: 0 10px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);"></iframe>`;

            const promptText = [
                'I need to recreate a beautiful UI component. Please generate the complete, working code for this component using HTML and Tailwind CSS.',
                '',
                'Here is the raw HTML structure for context:',
                '```html',
                rawHtml,
                '```',
                '',
                'Here is the custom CSS applied to it:',
                '```css',
                cssText.trim() === '/* No custom CSS needed. Pure Tailwind! */' ? '/* Pure Tailwind */' : cssText.trim(),
                '```',
                '',
                'Please output a single unified HTML file containing everything necessary (Tailwind via CDN, Google Fonts if any, CSS in a <style> tag, and the UI HTML). Ensure the design is pixel-perfect and uses the classes provided.'
            ].join('\n');
            currentModalData.prompt = promptText;
            
            switchTab('html');
        }

        function closeEmbedModal() {
            document.getElementById('embed-modal').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        function switchTab(tab) {
            currentTab = tab;
            const tabs = ['iframe', 'html', 'css', 'prompt'];
            tabs.forEach(t => {
                const btn = document.getElementById('tab-btn-' + t);
                if(t === tab) {
                    btn.classList.replace('border-transparent', 'border-indigo-600');
                    btn.classList.replace('text-gray-500', 'text-indigo-600');
                } else {
                    btn.classList.replace('border-indigo-600', 'border-transparent');
                    btn.classList.replace('text-indigo-600', 'text-gray-500');
                }
            });
            
            document.getElementById('embed-code-area').value = currentModalData[tab];
        }

        function copyCurrentCode() {
            const code = document.getElementById('embed-code-area').value;
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(code).then(() => {
                const btn = document.querySelector('#embed-modal .bg-white\\/10');
                const oldHTML = btn.innerHTML;
                btn.innerHTML = '<svg class="w-4 h-4 text-green-400" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path></svg> Copied!';
                setTimeout(() => { btn.innerHTML = oldHTML; }, 2000);
            });
        }

        // Mobile Menu Logic
        function toggleMobileMenu() {
            const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
            const backdrop = document.getElementById('mobile-backdrop');
            
            if (sidebar.classList.contains('-translate-x-full')) {
                sidebar.classList.remove('-translate-x-full');
                backdrop.classList.remove('hidden');
                setTimeout(() => backdrop.classList.remove('opacity-0'), 10);
                document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
            } else {
                sidebar.classList.add('-translate-x-full');
                backdrop.classList.add('opacity-0');
                setTimeout(() => backdrop.classList.add('hidden'), 300);
                document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';
            }
        }

        // Handle sidebar link clicks: smooth scroll and prevent URL hash change
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            // Remove hash on initial load if it exists so refresh doesn't auto-scroll
            if (window.location.hash) {
                setTimeout(() => {
                    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
                    history.replaceState(null, null, window.location.pathname);
                }, 10);
            }

            const sidebarLinks = document.querySelectorAll('#sidebar a');
            sidebarLinks.forEach(link => {
                link.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    
                    const targetId = this.getAttribute('href').substring(1);
                    const targetElement = document.getElementById(targetId);
                    
                    if (targetElement) {
                        targetElement.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
                    }

                    if (window.innerWidth < 1024) {
                        toggleMobileMenu();
                    }
                });
            });
        });

        // Search Functionality
        document.getElementById('search-input').addEventListener('input', function(e) {
            const searchTerm = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
            const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
            const detailsElements = sidebar.querySelectorAll('details');

            detailsElements.forEach(details => {
                const links = details.querySelectorAll('a');
                let hasVisibleLink = false;

                links.forEach(link => {
                    const text = link.textContent.toLowerCase();
                    const targetId = link.getAttribute('href').substring(1);
                    const targetSection = document.getElementById(targetId);
                    const sectionText = targetSection ? targetSection.textContent.toLowerCase() : '';
                    
                    if (text.includes(searchTerm) || sectionText.includes(searchTerm)) {
                        link.style.display = '';
                        hasVisibleLink = true;
                    } else {
                        link.style.display = 'none';
                    }
                });

                if (hasVisibleLink) {
                    details.style.display = '';
                    if (searchTerm !== '') {
                        details.setAttribute('open', '');
                    }
                } else {
                    details.style.display = 'none';
                }
            });
        });
    </script>
</body>

</html>
